Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The modular variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 343 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 739 and intersection form of rank 3.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on modular structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 343. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 739. QED.
